The advisor INSERTS this phase after Build on messaging cycles (scout.goal_type == "messaging") to prove that every changed message/event handler is safe to process the SAME message twice under at-least-once delivery — and BLOCKS when a handler double-processes with no dedup key, no exactly-once guard, and an unsafe replay. ([mickeyyaya/evolve-loop](https://github.com/mickeyyaya/evolve-loop/blob/HEAD/agents/evolve-idempotency-check.md) · ★7)
- **evolve-intent**: Pre-Scout intent capture agent. Takes a vague user goal, classifies it via the Ask-when-Needed framework, and emits a structured intent.md with goal/non-goals/constraints/interfaces/acceptance/assumptions/challenged-premises/risk-level. Mandatory ≥1 challenged premise. ([mickeyyaya/evolve-loop](https://github.com/mickeyyaya/evolve-loop/blob/HEAD/agents/evolve-intent.md) · ★7)
- **evolve-judge**: Blind scorer for /baransu:evolve, in three modes — pair (two neutrally-named SKILL.md versions on the fixed 9-dimension rubric, returning whether the change is a strict improvement; 3 in parallel per round), rank (lettered seed bedrocks, ranked against the round's brief), and exam (benchmark outputs under neutral labels, scored against the answer keys). Never knows which version is the mutation or which output came from which version. ([TLOGBen/baransu](https://github.com/TLOGBen/baransu/blob/HEAD/plugins/baransu/agents/evolve-judge.md))
- **evolve-license-provenance-audit**: License & provenance audit agent for the Evolve Loop (Evaluate archetype). The advisor INSERTS this phase after Build whenever the cycle's scout.goal_type == "supply-chain", to vet every newly added or version-bumped dependency for license compatibility and provenance integrity before it reaches the tree. ([mickeyyaya/evolve-loop](https://github.com/mickeyyaya/evolve-loop/blob/HEAD/agents/evolve-license-provenance-audit.md) · ★7)
- **evolve-locale-format-check**: Internationalization audit agent for the Evolve Loop (Evaluate archetype). The advisor INSERTS this phase after Build whenever the cycle's scout.goal_type == "i18n" to verify changed UI/string code externalizes copy and formats data locale-aware before launch. ([mickeyyaya/evolve-loop](https://github.com/mickeyyaya/evolve-loop/blob/HEAD/agents/evolve-locale-format-check.md) · ★7)
- **evolve-market-sizing**: Market sizing and feasibility analysis agent for the Evolve Loop (Evaluate archetype). Estimates TAM, SAM, and SOM, outlining methodology and assumptions. ([mickeyyaya/evolve-loop](https://github.com/mickeyyaya/evolve-loop/blob/HEAD/agents/evolve-market-sizing.md) · ★7)
- **evolve-memo**: Lightweight PASS-cycle memo agent (v8.57.0+, Layer P). Fires on PASS verdicts after ship to capture observations Scout/Triage saw but did NOT commit to top_n. Single-pass, ~$0.10–0.20 per cycle. Emits carryover-todos.json and memo.md — does NOT do retrospective/digest work (that's the FAIL/WARN retrospective agent's job). - **evolve-merge-to-main-gate**: Merge-to-main promotion-readiness gate for the Evolve Loop (Evaluate archetype). The campaign executor invokes this at milestone/wave boundaries to verify a completed milestone's already-integrated work is ready to be promoted to main, emitting a PASS/WARN/FAIL verdict plus merge_gate.* signals. READ-ONLY — it never merges, never edits source; the kernel promoter acts on the verdict. - **evolve-observability-design**: Observability-design agent for the Evolve Loop (Plan archetype). - **evolve-orchestrator**: Cycle orchestrator subagent. Déclenche en quatrième étape du pipeline cert, ou quand l'utilisateur dit "génère mes fiches de révision", "quelles questions vais-je avoir", "prépare-moi à l'examen", "donne-moi les questions probables". ([YoLaub/skills_stock](https://github.com/YoLaub/skills_stock/blob/HEAD/agents/exam-preparer.md) · ★1)
- **exam-readness-code-quality-auditor**: Strict senior software quality auditor for D:\projects\exam-readness-tuttor: SOLID, composition over inheritance, uv dependency hygiene, validation, logging, security, tests, and maintainability. Use after material edits, before publishing, or when the user asks for standards compliance. ([Julio-M-Siqueira/agents-handbook](https://github.com/Julio-M-Siqueira/agents-handbook/blob/HEAD/projects/exam-readness-tuttor/agents/exam-readness-code-quality-auditor.md))
- **exam-release**: &gt; 2026-10-30 루트 `AGENTS.md`에서 옮긴 원문이다. 문구는 그대로 두고, 링크 경로만 이 폴더 기준으로 고쳤다. 본문 속 백틱 경로는 저장소 루트 기준이다. &gt; 이 영역 작업을 시작할 때 읽는다. 공통 규칙과 작업별 안내는 루트 [AGENTS.md](../../AGENTS.md)에 있다. 새 지시는 루트 `AGENTS.md`가 아니라 이 문서에 추가한다. ([icefoxtail/AP------](https://github.com/icefoxtail/AP------/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/exam-release.md))
- **exam-reviewer**: 수능 직업탐구 블라인드 검토위원. 정답을 모르는 수험생으로서 review-brief.md만 보고 문항을 풀고 결함을 찾아 review JSON을 저장한다. 여러 문항을 한 번에 맡을 수 있다. ([officeshinyujun/gap-all](https://github.com/officeshinyujun/gap-all/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/exam-reviewer.md))
- **exam-reviewer**: Experienced examiner who turns a run's statistics and feedback analysis into concrete, prioritized edits to the exam, each backed by evidence. Used by the /mock-exam workflow. ([Tonidor/ai-for-research](https://github.com/Tonidor/ai-for-research/blob/HEAD/mock-exam/.claude/agents/exam-reviewer.md))
- **exam-stem-author**: Specialised subagent for authoring KFP and MCQ practice stems in RACGP / ACRRM / AMC style. Pre-loads distractor-design, examiner-feedback-mining, and kfp-failure-bucket so it produces stems that match the AU specificity bar without being told. Invoke when the caller wants a new stem, a stem rewrite, or a stem-plus-marking-key for a named topic. ([anon2023-Halmoni/claude-fleet-marketplace](https://github.com/anon2023-Halmoni/claude-fleet-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/smp-kat-tools/agents/exam-stem-author.md))
- **exam-study-coach**: University exam preparation coach specializing in active recall, Anki spaced-repetition flashcards, practice quizzes, and step-by-step problem walkthroughs. ([Osama-Alzahrani-UQU/OmniAgentic](https://github.com/Osama-Alzahrani-UQU/OmniAgentic/blob/HEAD/agents/exam-study-coach.md) · ★2)
- **exam-validator**: mock-exam セクションの quiz.csv を検証する。①CSV整合性 ②Domain列のドメイン配分がノルマ通りか ③問題タイプ比率 ④ブループリント外（シラバス外）の問題が無いか ⑤question-bank と突き合わせ本横断の重複が無いか ⑥出典がドメインのドキュメント領域内か・正答と食い違っていないか を判定し、退避とインデックス更新を行う。 ([nomhiro/shiro-plugin-marketplace](https://github.com/nomhiro/shiro-plugin-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/udemy-exam-prep/agents/exam-validator.md))
- **exam-video-maker**: Produces a finished explainer video for one exam question folder (screenshot + script.txt + narration.mp3, e.g. my/q4) following the series guideline, and reports the result. Use when the user asks to make or redo a question video and is happy to delegate the whole job. ([NethsaraWMRC/whiteboard-animation-maker](https://github.com/NethsaraWMRC/whiteboard-animation-maker/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/exam-video-maker.md))
- **exam-writer**: 수능 직업탐구(성공적인 직업생활·공업 일반) 문항 출제위원. exam-question-generator 스킬의 작업 폴더에서 맡은 문항 하나만 brief.md대로 쓰고 엔진 check를 통과시킨다. 블라인드 검토는 하지 않는다. ([officeshinyujun/gap-all](https://github.com/officeshinyujun/gap-all/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/exam-writer.md))
- **exam_agent**: You are the **Exam Agent** for the live-tutor skill. You handle the final comprehensive exam. ([xRayyyyyyy/live-tutor](https://github.com/xRayyyyyyy/live-tutor/blob/HEAD/agents/exam_agent.md))
- **exam_qa:author-fixture**: Build an ExamFixture from a topic outline or transcript. Produces a JSON-or-YAML-shaped fixture with deterministic question IDs, prompts, expected answers, and tags. No grading or scenario binding happens here. ([xninetzy-labs/xninetzy](https://github.com/xninetzy-labs/xninetzy/blob/HEAD/.agents/skills/exam-qa/agents/author-fixture.md) · ★5 · 2 copies)
- **exam_qa:run-dry-practice**: Run a practice or dry-run scenario against a fixture, scoring each question with the local heuristic provider. Produces evidence bundles and a graded ExamRunState. Always idempotent — replays return the same run_id. ([xninetzy-labs/xninetzy](https://github.com/xninetzy-labs/xninetzy/blob/HEAD/.agents/skills/exam-qa/agents/run-dry-practice.md) · ★5 · 2 copies)
- **examen-final-defendible**: Diseña una evaluación final defendible para un curso, junto con la redacción del certificado: competencias evaluadas, diseño de la tarea, criterios de aprobado, proceso de reclamación y afirmaciones honestas. Úsalo cuando un curso vaya a emitir un certificado y la prueba final deba resistir un escrutinio. ([Nawii99/arsenal-piezas](https://github.com/Nawii99/arsenal-piezas/blob/HEAD/plugins/educacion-alternativas-3/agents/examen-final-defendible.md))
- **examen-final-y-certificado**: Úsalo para diseñar la evaluación final de un programa de formación: un proyecto o examen final, los criterios de aprobado, la guía de corrección, la redacción del certificado y lo que el certificado puede afirmar con honestidad. Úsalo también para revisar una certificación existente que nadie se toma en serio. ([Nawii99/arsenal-piezas](https://github.com/Nawii99/arsenal-piezas/blob/HEAD/plugins/educacion-evaluacion/agents/examen-final-y-certificado.md))
- **examinador**: Examinador do time, papel fixo e único gate dos tickets com código. Use para revisar (/revisao) código contra os CA e o Detalhe, checar padrões, ADR e DS, e rodar a suíte. No passe de documentação, revisa tickets também só de texto apenas para rodar as guardas Documentacao. Dá APROVADO só com nota ≥ 80%, zero achados no código e guardas verdes. Não implementa. ([gabrielctorres/SpellBook](https://github.com/gabrielctorres/SpellBook/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/examinador.md))
- **examinador-sustentacion**: Simula la sustentación oral (45 % de la nota). Hace preguntas como lo haría la profesora sobre las dos reducciones, las cotas y la complejidad, y evalúa la respuesta del integrante. Úsalo para ensayar antes de la sustentación. Solo lee, nunca edita. ([Makkima16/ADA_2026B](https://github.com/Makkima16/ADA_2026B/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/examinador-sustentacion.md))
- **examination-prep-specialist**: Use for regulator examination readiness — exam PBC list management, walkthrough rehearsals, mock examiner interviews, examiner Q&A drafting, MRA / MRIA / management-letter responses, and remediation tracking. Spawn weeks before an exam or on a request. NOT for financial-statement audit prep. ([mcorbett51090/RavenClaude](https://github.com/mcorbett51090/RavenClaude/blob/HEAD/plugins/regulatory-compliance/agents/examination-prep-specialist.md) · ★9)
- **examine-advisor**: Examines a decision (a set of options) or a conceptual document (PRD, tech-design, etc.) in a clean context, then ADVISES: recommends which option to pick when the choice is unclear or too technical, or finds inconsistencies and suggests concrete improvements in a document. Run in two passes (research + internal) by the dev:research:examine command, which reconciles them. Never edits the target. Also use ad hoc, outside that command, whenever a decision or a document needs a clean-context second opinion this session's author cannot give. ([povesma/embo](https://github.com/povesma/embo/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/examine-advisor.md) · ★13)
- **examine-compat-validator**: Use this agent when the user asks to validate Examine v4 code changes against v3 API compatibility or ensure backward compatibility with Examine v3. Trigger phrases include: - 'check v3 compatibility' - 'will this break v3 APIs?' - 'validate backward compatibility' - 'ensure v3 compatibility' - 'is this a breaking change?' - 'check API contract compatibility' Examples: - User says 'I modified the searcher API—will this break v3 compatibility?' → invoke this agent to analyze the change against v3 contracts - User asks 'before we release, check that this change is backward compatible with v3' →… ([Shazwazza/Examine](https://github.com/Shazwazza/Examine/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/examine-compat-validator.agent.md) · ★409)
- **examine-worker**: Producer's examine worker — examines a lint-passed report and determines one thing only — is the material sufficient enough to become an approach? It never judges the correctness of the idea. Cap 8 minutes. Read-only; primary model (quality-critical gate). ([zhongganpage/Spell-Explore](https://github.com/zhongganpage/Spell-Explore/blob/HEAD/skills/spell-explore/references/protocol/.kimi-code/agents/examine-worker.md) · ★3)
- **examiner**: Mock exam creator. Designs mock exams that match the real exam in format, difficulty, and timing, and writes detailed answer explanations. ([revfactory/harness-100](https://github.com/revfactory/harness-100/blob/HEAD/en/57-exam-prep/.claude/agents/examiner.md) · ★1.3k · 3 copies)
- **examiner**: 모의고사 출제자. 실전과 동일한 형식·난이도·시간의 모의고사를 출제하고, 상세한 해설을 작성한다. ([revfactory/harness-100](https://github.com/revfactory/harness-100/blob/HEAD/ko/57-exam-prep/.claude/agents/examiner.md) · ★1.3k · 2 copies)
- **examiner**: Widget Factory examiner. Holds the ideation gate; rejects ideas that are variations on existing widgets. ([oMaN-Rod/claude-code-widgets](https://github.com/oMaN-Rod/claude-code-widgets/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/examiner.md) · ★5)
- **examiner**: Generates CBSE-style board questions by chapter, marks, and difficulty. Follows official CBSE format — MCQ, Assertion-Reasoning, SA, LA, Case-Based. ([Vishnu-tppr/everything-cbse-board](https://github.com/Vishnu-tppr/everything-cbse-board/blob/HEAD/10th/agents/examiner.md) · ★3)
- **examiner**: CBSE 12th Board Examiner Agent — Generates questions matching the 70/80 mark paper patterns. Includes MCQs, Assertion-Reason, Case-Based, and Long Answer Derivations. ([Vishnu-tppr/everything-cbse-board](https://github.com/Vishnu-tppr/everything-cbse-board/blob/HEAD/12th/agents/examiner.md) · ★3)
- **examiner**: Проверяет удержание по завершённым модулям — задаёт вопросы скептически, не подыгрывая, ловит дыры понимания. Свежий взгляд: НЕ объясняет в процессе, НЕ подсказывает ответ. ([temperalo/study-world-history-3](https://github.com/temperalo/study-world-history-3/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/examiner.md) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **examiner**: Anchors a Celestial Court trial — interrogates the user to extract maximum requirements, constraints, goals, and success criteria, then writes anchor.md. Use proactively at the start of a Celestial Court case. ([LokeshDandumahanti/Celestial_Court](https://github.com/LokeshDandumahanti/Celestial_Court/blob/HEAD/agents/examiner.md) · ★1)
- **examiner**: The Academy EXAMINER: writes an exam (missions, fixtures, checks) for a skill from what the skill promises, not from how it is built. Dispatched by the enroll skill. ([omer-yehuda/plugins](https://github.com/omer-yehuda/plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/academy/agents/examiner.md) · ★1)
- **examiner**: Generates the exercise layer — competency-gated exercises, spaced repetition metadata, abstraction prompts, and boss levels. Produces assessments that require genuine understanding, not pattern matching. Invoked by the coordinator during full generation. ([psreek-ai/codebase-mooc](https://github.com/psreek-ai/codebase-mooc/blob/HEAD/agents/examiner.md) · ★1)
- **examiner**: Беспристрастный экзаменатор mentor (строг к сути, не к словам; учитывает purpose курса). Проверяет ответы ученика вслепую (без переписки урока) по рубрике и ключам, код — запуском, расчёты — пересчётом. Возвращает только JSON-вердикт mentor_grade. Используй для проверки на выходе урока, квизов, диагностики и экзаменов. ([zarabotaet/mentor](https://github.com/zarabotaet/mentor/blob/HEAD/mentor/agents/examiner.md) · ★1)
- **examiner**: Genera prompt di active recall coerenti con l'ultima unità erogata da tutor, valuta la risposta del learner secondo il testing effect (prima interroga, poi dà feedback), e propone card Anki per gli errori quando un Anki MCP è connesso. Invocato da tutor a chiusura di ogni sessione di /learn e durante /review, non direttamente dall'utente. ([alesop95/discoteca-api](https://github.com/alesop95/discoteca-api/blob/HEAD/.claude/templates/learning-agent/agents/examiner.md))
- **examiner**: Fair examiner — creates curriculum-aligned tests, marks with detailed feedback ([cogentglobal/tutorific-plugin](https://github.com/cogentglobal/tutorific-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/examiner.md))
- **examiner**: Thesis DSRM row 11d. Reads one drafted section as the thesis committee would and lists the questions it would ask at the defense. Dispatched by the dsrm-6-communicate skill. ([GarroteSantiago/Thesis](https://github.com/GarroteSantiago/Thesis/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/examiner.md))
